<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解嵌入量子点的超导环的基态性质，尤其是混合价态区域中有限U安德森杂质与超导环耦合导致的量子相变及超流响应。

Method: 采用变分波函数方法，建立了有限U安德森杂质与超导环耦合的理论处理框架。

Result: 证明了系统在量子点处于混合价态区域时存在单重态-二重态基态转变；超电流随外加磁通振荡，并在单重态-二重态相变点处呈现突然跳变。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了超导环-量子点系统中基态自旋转变与超电流的可观测关联，为相关相变行为提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the ground state properties of a superconducting ring embedded with a quantum dot (QD) by using a variational wave-function approach. A theoretical formulation for the treatment of the finite-U Anderson impurity coupled with a superconducting ring are presented. We demonstrate singlet-doublet transitions of the ground state for this system with the QD in the mixed valence regime. It is shown that the supercurrent in the superconductor ring shows oscillations with the external enclosed magnetic flux and exhibits abrupt jumps at the singlet-doublet phase transition points.

</details>


### [95] [Scalar Spin Chirality from Dissipative Pumping and Lamb Shift Precession](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30324)
*YuanDong Wang,JianHua Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 揭示三角三量子点中循环电流在零磁通和实哈密顿量下随偏压反复反转的机制：耗散隧穿制备轨道赝自旋，虚电荷涨落产生非共线Lamb场驱动其进动，经轨道Hanle效应将非手征极化转化为手征性，从而产生循环电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角三量子点中循环电流在零磁通和实哈密顿量下随偏压反复反转，这一现象起源长期未明，作者旨在给出其微观机制。

Method: 采用理论分析与层级运动方程计算相结合。提出耗散隧穿制备轨道赝自旋，虚电荷涨落产生非共线Lamb场使其向手征方向旋转；偏压改变两者相对取向，进而调控电流。利用精确基态多重态恒等式将非手征轨道极化与标量自旋手征性及循环电流联系。

Result: 低偏压下的电流反转在超越二阶弱耦合的层级运动方程计算中依然存在；电压突然切换后手征性的二次增长表明其对应进动而非纯耗散行为。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统中时间反演奇响应可由耗散态制备与相干进动的相互作用产生，为理解此类现象提供统一物理图像。

Abstract: Circulating currents in triangular triple quantum dots reverse repeatedly with bias even at zero magnetic flux and for a real Hamiltonian, a phenomenon whose origin has remained unclear. We show that dissipative tunneling prepares an orbital pseudospin, while virtual charge fluctuations generate a noncollinear Lamb field that rotates it toward the chiral direction. Bias changes their relative orientation and thereby reverses the current. This reservoir-induced orbital Hanle effect converts a nonchiral orbital polarization into scalar spin chirality, which an exact ground-multiplet identity links to the circulating current. Hierarchical-equations-of-motion calculations show that the low-bias reversal survives beyond the second-order weak-coupling description, while the quadratic growth of chirality after a sudden voltage switch identifies precession. More broadly, time-reversal-odd responses can emerge in open quantum systems from the interplay between dissipative state preparation and coherent precession.

</details>


### [96] [Exact fluctuation relations in voltage- and temperature-biased Laughlin-edge constrictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30535)
*Gu Zhang,Gabriele Campagnano,Domenico Giuliano,Igor Gornyi,In`es Safi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 对非平衡涨落-耗散关系进行了全面分析，证明在任意隧穿强度、电压偏置和边缘态温度下，QPC处的隧穿电流噪声可由下游测量的手征电流自相关和互相关精确重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上直接测量QPC处的局域隧穿电流噪声较为困难，需要从下游可观测的手征电流关联中提取非平衡输运信息，因此探索连接两者的涨落-耗散关系。

Method: 理论分析两个处于不同温度和电压下的手征Laughlin边缘态经QPC耦合体系，推导手征电流自/互相关与隧穿电流噪声及电导之间的关系，并推广至空间扩展QPC及具有显式电压依赖的隧穿振幅情形。

Result: 这些涨落-耗散关系在任意隧穿强度、偏压和边缘态温度下均严格成立；推广至更一般情形后需讨论其适用条件与限制，但局域隧穿噪声仍可由下游关联可靠重建。

Conclusion: 建立了从下游关联测量表征手征边缘态非平衡输运的鲁棒路径，为实验提供了可行的重构方案。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of non-equilibrium fluctuation-dissipation relations connect- ing experimentally accessible chiral-current auto- and cross-correlations to the tunneling-current noise and conductance in Laughlin edge states coupled through a quantum point contact (QPC). We examine their validity for two chiral Laughlin edges held at different temperatures and voltages and show that the relations remain exact for arbitrary tunneling strength, voltage bias, and edge- state temperatures. We further generalize them to spatially extended QPCs and to tunneling am- plitudes with an explicit voltage dependence, and discuss the conditions and limitations associated with these generalizations. Our results establish that the local tunneling-current noise generated at the QPC can be reliably reconstructed from experimentally accessible auto- and cross-correlations measured downstream, providing a robust route to characterize non-equilibrium transport in chiral edge states.

</details>


### [97] [Dynamics of a nanoscale ferromagnetic vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30511)
*Jun Seok Seo,Se Kwon Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种由界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用稳定的纳米铁磁涡旋，其尺寸可小于5 nm，并通过理论分析与微磁模拟揭示其非线性动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统铁磁涡旋在纳米盘中的稳定需要约微米量级的半径，限制了器件的小型化，因此需要探索在更小尺度下稳定涡旋的机制。

Method: 采用理论分析与微磁模拟相结合的方法，解析求解描述非简谐涡旋振荡的非线性运动方程，并识别决定驱动振荡稳定性的临界频率。

Result: 该涡旋在半径低于5 nm的纳米盘中仍保持稳定，远小于传统涡旋；同时表现出常规的回转振荡和垂直磁场下的共振频移，以及强非简谐势、非线性振荡和类似Duffing振子的响应。

Conclusion: 该纳米涡旋兼具传统涡旋的基本特性与独特的非线性行为，为超小型自旋电子学器件提供新的可能。

Abstract: We propose a ferromagnetic vortex stabilized by the interfacial Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction (iDMI) and investigate its properties through theoretical analysis and micromagnetic simulations. Our results demonstrate that this vortex can remain stable even in nanoscale ferromagnetic disks with radii below $5\,\text{nm}$---far smaller than those of conventional nanodot vortices having about $1\,μ\text{m}$ radius. We analytically solve the nonlinear equation of motion describing the anharmonic vortex oscillation, and identify the critical frequency that determines the stability of the driven oscillation of the vortex. This nanoscale vortex exhibits conventional properties of microscale vortices, including gyrotropic oscillation and resonance frequency shift under an out-of-plane magnetic field. It also exhibits unconventional behaviors, such as a strongly anharmonic potential, nonlinear oscillations, and a Duffing-oscillator-like response under the external AC bias.

</details>


### [98] [Moiré-induced lattice reconstruction at buried atomic interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30531)
*Nicholas Clark,Frederick Allars,Isaac Soltero,Wendong Wang,David Hopkinson,Hugo de Latour,James G. McHugh,William Talbott,Sam Sullivan-Allsop,Rongsheng Cai,Astrid Weston,Xiao Li,Gareth Tainton,Casey K. Cheung,Francisco Selles,Alex Summerfield,Andrey Kretinin,Christopher S. Allen,Vladimir Falko,Sarah J. Haigh,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用多切片电子叠层衍射技术，非侵入式恢复了厚过渡金属二硫化物晶体间微扭转菱面体界面的三维原子结构，揭示了界面重构和位错网络，并发现面内重构强于三维模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转二维界面的原子重构决定许多涌现性质，但现有研究多集中于悬浮双层体系，多层晶体之间的异质界面结构变化尚不清楚。

Method: 采用多切片电子叠层衍射（multi-slice electron ptychography）实现非侵入式三维原子成像，具备约3 pm的位置精度和约1 nm的深度分辨率，逐层解析扭转诱导的晶格重构场及界面位错网络。

Result: 在体材料中仍观察到显著强于三维模型预测的面内界面重构，原因在于外层晶体抑制了面外弯曲；同时提取了重构向体内衰减过程中的应变张量演化。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解多层莫尔体系的结构基础提供了直接实验证据，表明体相约束会增强界面重构，需修正现有三维模型。

Abstract: Atomic reconstruction at twisted two-dimensional interfaces governs many of their emergent optical, electronic, and mechanical properties, including sliding ferroelectricity. Despite recent progress in understanding lattice reconstruction in suspended twisted bilayers, structural changes at van der Waals heterointerfaces between multilayer crystals remain largely unexplored. Here we use multi-slice electron ptychography to non-invasively recover the three-dimensional atomic structure at marginally twisted rhombohedral interfaces between thick transition-metal dichalcogenide crystals. With a position precision of ~3 pm and a depth resolution ~1 nm, we resolve the twist-induced lattice reconstruction field per layer, and the resulting dislocation network at the buried interface. Despite the bulk nature, we observe markedly strong in-plane interfacial reconstruction due to suppression of the out-of-plane bending by outer layers, exceeding predictions from our three-dimensional modelling. Furthermore, we extract the strain tensor evolution during the decay of the reconstruction into the bulk, providing a structural foundation for understanding multi-layer moiré systems.

</details>


### [99] [Predictive wavelength tailoring of uniform GaSb-based quantum dots for emission at 1.55 um](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30628)
*Markus Peil,Maja Wasiluk,Ziemowit Olinkiewicz,Tymon Przychodni,Robert Matysiak,Teemu Taskinen,Joona Salonen,Abhiroop Chellu,Metin Patli,Joonas Hilska,Anna Musiał,Michał Gawełczyk,Mircea Guina,Teemu Hakkarainen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 系统研究了基于GaSb的液滴刻蚀纳米孔洞中InGaSb填充形成的量子点（LDE QDs）的发射波长调控，通过独立调节量子点组分和尺寸，将发射波长从1.48 μm调至电信C波段中心（1.55 μm），并实现了极窄的均匀展宽与单量子点精细结构分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为长距离光纤网络中的量子光子应用寻找可调谐、高均匀性的电信波段量子点平台，而GaSb基LDE量子点在C波段发射上具有潜力但缺乏系统的波长调控研究。

Method: 通过InGaSb填充液滴刻蚀的AlGaSb纳米孔洞制备量子点，独立改变In含量（组分）和填充单层数（ML，尺寸），结合光学表征与考虑均匀In组分、低互混及显微实测几何结构的仿真模拟，分析发射能量移动规律；并利用单量子点光致发光测量精细结构分裂。

Result: 发射能量随In含量线性红移，斜率-4.4 meV/In%，随ML数线性移动，斜率-2.0 meV/ML；仿真预测分别为-4.3 meV/In%和-2.1 meV/ML，与实验高度吻合。所有样品的非均匀展宽远低于7 meV，单量子点激子线宽13.8±6.7 μeV，精细结构分裂值多数<10 μeV。

Conclusion: GaSb基LDE量子点被证实为一种可调谐的电信平台，兼具优异的均匀性和低精细结构分裂，适合用于长距离光纤网络中的可扩展量子光子应用。

Abstract: A detailed study of emission wavelength tailoring of GaSb-based QDs formed by InGaSb-filling of droplet-etched nanoholes in AlGaSb is presented. The study shows that the emission wavelength can be modified from 1.48 um to the center of the telecom C-band at 1.55 mm by independently varying the QD composition and size. More specifically, the optical transition energy shifts linearly as a function of In-content of the QD material at a rate of -4.4 meV/In-percentage, and with the number of monolayers (ML) of material used for filling the nanoholes, at -2.0 meV/ML. These experimentally observed energy shifts are well predicted by simulations yielding rates of -4.3 meV/In-percentage and -2.1 meV/ML, respectively. For the simulation, a uniform In composition, low intermixing, and microscopically measured QD geometry is considered. Additionally, excellent ensemble QD uniformity, with unprecedented inhomogeneous broadening well-below 7 meV across all samples is demonstrated. Finally, photoluminescence of single-QDs reveals narrow excitonic emission lines of 13.8+/-6.7 ueV and low fine-structure splitting values reaching <10 ueV. These results identify GaSb-based LDE QDs as a tunable telecom platform for scaling quantum-photonic applications over long-haul optical fiber networks.

</details>


### [100] [Coexistence of polariton bound states in the continuum and 2D radiative excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30691)
*Simone Zanotti,Marco Liscidini,Dario Gerace,Lucio C. Andreani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明，将激子辐射损耗纳入非厄米 Hopfield 形式后，能够正确描述 k=0 处具有发散辐射寿命的真正极化激元连续谱束缚态（BIC），从而为辐射激子与极化激元 BIC 提供统一的微观理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续谱束缚态（BIC）可提供理想无限辐射寿命的光学模式，而周期性 patterned 平面波导中嵌入二维激子体系可用 Hopfield 矩阵描述。然而，二维激子在光锥内具有本征的波矢依赖辐射衰减，Hopfield 形式是否能自洽描述这一过程并保持极化激元 BIC 的存在，此前尚不清楚。

Method: 采用包含独立光子与激子损耗的非厄米 Hopfield 形式，并明确纳入二维激子的辐射损耗通道，通过系统求解本征问题来分析 k=0 极化激元 BIC 的存在性与辐射寿命行为。

Result: 发现该非厄米 Hopfield 形式能够正确捕获真正的 k=0 极化激元 BIC，其辐射寿命发散，说明激子辐射损耗与 BIC 的保持可以自洽共存。

Conclusion: 该理论为辐射激子和极化激元 BIC 提供了统一的微观描述，并可作为高效的预测工具，用于设计耦合量子阱、过渡金属硫化物及其他二维激子材料的光子晶体平台。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) enable optical modes with ideally infinite radiative lifetimes despite lying within the radiation continuum. Radiation-matter interaction in periodically patterned planar waveguides embedding two-dimensional (2D) optically active excitations can be described quantum mechanically by diagonalizing a non-Hermitian operator, known as the Hopfield matrix, which can be generalized to incorporate independent photonic and excitonic losses into the polaritonic states. However, since 2D excitons undergo intrinsic wavevector-dependent radiative decay within the light cone, whether the Hopfield formalism can consistently account for this process while preserving polariton BICs has remained an open question. Here we show that a non-Hermitian Hopfield formalism incorporating excitonic radiative losses correctly captures the existence of genuine $k=0$ polariton BICs with diverging radiative lifetimes. The theory provides a unified microscopic framework for radiative excitons and polariton BICs, and an efficient predictive tool for designing photonic-crystal platforms coupled to quantum wells, transition-metal dichalcogenides, and other 2D excitonic materials.

</details>


### [101] [Supermoiré Reconstruction and Topological Mosaics in Twisted Trilayer WSe$_2$ and MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30813)
*Hai Meng,Yang Xu,Fengcheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用机器学习力场和大规模从头计算，研究了螺旋与交替扭转三层WSe2/MoTe2中的晶格弛豫和能带结构，发现双层moire干涉形成的supermoire在弛豫后重构为少数局域可公度的双层moire畴；由于体系缺乏C2z对称性，这些畴在能量和拓扑上彼此不等价，最高价带来自K/K'谷且具有畴依赖的谷陈数，构成拓扑不等价畴的马赛克平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转三层过渡金属硫族化合物中两个双层moire晶格之间的干涉会产生supermoire结构，现有研究多集中于三层石墨烯，而缺乏对缺少C2z对称性的TMD体系在弛豫与拓扑性质上的系统理解。

Method: 采用机器学习力场进行大尺度结构弛豫模拟，并结合大规模ab initio计算分析晶格重构、局域堆垛识别以及能带拓扑性质。

Result: supermoire在弛豫后重构为少数主导畴类型，各畴具有局域可公度的双层moire；因缺少C2z对称性，原本由该对称性关联的畴在能量和拓扑上分化；顶价带来源于K/K'谷，不同畴具有不同的谷陈数。

Conclusion: 该体系形成由拓扑不等价畴构成的supermoire马赛克，为研究关联电子和拓扑物理提供了新的材料平台。

Abstract: We investigate lattice relaxation and band structures of helical and alternating twisted trilayer WSe$_2$ and MoTe$_2$ using machine-learning force fields and large-scale ab initio calculations. Interference between the two bilayer moiré lattices generates a supermoiré lattice that, upon relaxation, reconstructs into a few dominant domain types with locally commensurate bilayer moiré lattices. Because the systems lack $C_{2z}$ symmetry, domains otherwise related by this symmetry become energetically and topologically distinct, unlike in twisted trilayer graphene. Band structure calculations show that the topmost valence bands originate from the $K$ ($K'$) valleys and carry domain-dependent valley Chern numbers. The resulting supermoiré lattice hosts a mosaic of topologically inequivalent domains, offering a platform for exploring correlated and topological physics.

</details>


### [102] [Local phase-space Berry curvature and Hall transport in textured twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30882)
*Tohid Farajollahpour*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究扭转双层石墨烯中慢转角与异质应变织构对相空间Berry几何的影响，指出纯几何tetrad/shift扇区在一致处理自旋联络时不产生混合Berry曲率，而投影的织构化迷你狄拉克锥则具有真实的混合相空间曲率。在一维织构和镜像M_x对称的局域Hall棒极限下，赝规范梯度仅重正化沿织构方向的纵向电导，非线性直流Hall响应仅在额外的谷奇倾斜产生Berry曲率偶极时出现。两种几何响应在局域填充μ_loc=√2 m处同时达到峰值，提供栅压可调的共同起源指纹。所有局域锥参数和织构磁化率均从θ=1.3°的异质应变Bistritzer-MacDonald模型中提取，所需倾斜对应的异质应变仅0.03%-0.2%，低于器件中常规成像值。该理论为织构化moire狄拉克材料提供了局域解析框架，并清晰分离了几何、织构诱导和输运层面的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层石墨烯中的慢转角织构和异质应变织构被视为实现相空间Berry几何的自然途径，但此前对纯几何扇区是否产生混合Berry曲率以及织构如何影响输运性质缺乏一致理解。本文旨在澄清自旋联络处理对混合Berry曲率的影响，并建立织构化迷你狄拉克锥的相空间几何响应与可观测输运信号之间的联系。

Method: 采用局域解析方法，首先论证在一致处理自旋联络时纯tetrad/shift扇区不产生混合Berry曲率；随后考虑投影的织构化迷你狄拉克锥，推导其混合相空间曲率。在一维织构且具有镜像M_x对称性的局域Hall棒极限下，分析赝规范梯度对纵向电导的修正及非线性Hall响应的条件，引入谷奇倾斜以产生Berry曲率偶极。利用异质应变的Bistritzer-MacDonald模型在θ=1.3°提取局域锥参数和织构磁化率，并通过输运估计确定对应的异质应变范围。

Result: 纯几何tetrad/shift扇区在自旋联络一致处理下不产生混合Berry曲率；而织构化迷你狄拉克锥会产生真实的混合相空间曲率。在一维织构和M_x对称性下，赝规范梯度只重正化沿织构方向的纵向电导，直流非线性Hall响应仅由谷奇倾斜诱导的Berry曲率偶极产生。两种几何响应在局域填充μ_loc=√2 m处共同达到峰值。从Bistritzer-MacDonald模型提取的参数表明，输运估计所需的倾斜仅对应0.03%-0.2%的异质应变，远低于实验常规成像值。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了织构化moire狄拉克材料的局域解析框架，能够清晰区分几何、织构诱导和输运层面的贡献。混合Berry曲率并非来自纯几何扇区，而是源于投影的织构化迷你狄拉克锥。局域填充处的共同峰值和低异质应变要求为实验探测提供了可行途径，且该理论对实际TBG器件中的织构效应具有直接适用性。

Abstract: Slow twist-angle and heterostrain textures in twisted bilayer graphene provide a natural route to phase-space Berry geometry. We show that a purely geometric tetrad/shift sector does not generate mixed Berry curvature once the spin connection is treated consistently. By contrast, a projected textured mini-Dirac cone in twisted bilayer graphene acquires genuine mixed phase-space curvature. We analyze a controlled local Hall-bar limit with a one-dimensional texture and mirror $M_x$. In that limit the pseudogauge gradient renormalizes only the longitudinal conductivity along the textured direction, while a dc nonlinear Hall response appears only when an additional valley-odd tilt generates a Berry-curvature dipole. Both geometric responses peak at the same local filling, $μ_{\rm loc}=\sqrt{2}\,m$, providing a gate-tunable fingerprint of their common origin. All local-cone parameters and their texture susceptibilities are extracted from a heterostrained Bistritzer-MacDonald model at $θ=1.3^\circ$. The tilt invoked in the transport estimates corresponds to heterostrain of only $0.03\%-0.2\%$, below values routinely imaged in devices. The resulting theory provides a local analytic framework for textured moiré Dirac materials and cleanly separates geometric, texture-induced, and transport-level ingredients relevant to realistic TBG.

</details>


### [103] [Signatures of inter-sideband coherence in the resonance fluorescence spectrum of an acoustically-modulated quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30886)
*Rafał A. Bogaczewicz,Hubert J. Krenner,Paweł Machnikowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过滤波场形式理论分析了马赫-曾德尔构型中声学调制量子点共振荧光谱的侧带间相位相干性，发现干涉图的几何倾斜度是抗环境白噪声的相干性指标；噪声导致的谱扩散仅降低整体条纹强度，不改变特征倾斜角，为频域编码和可扩展量子网络中的单光子侧带相干验证提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固态量子点系统中，频率分箱编码和可扩展量子网络需要验证光谱侧带之间的单光子相位相干性；然而环境噪声，尤其是白噪声引起的谱扩散，会破坏干涉可见度，使得传统相干性判据不可靠，因此需要寻找对噪声免疫的相干性度量。

Method: 采用滤波场形式描述共振荧光，并针对马赫-曾德尔干涉配置计算声学调制量子点发射的干涉图；分析噪声引起的谱扩散对全局条纹强度和干涉图几何倾斜度的影响，从而检验相位相干性指标的稳健性。

Result: 干涉图的几何倾斜度可作为侧带间相位相干性的可靠指示器：环境白噪声只降低全局条纹强度，而特征倾斜度保持不变；这证明该度量对噪声引起的谱扩散具有内在不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在真实噪声固态体系中验证单光子侧带间相位相干性的理论框架，为频率分箱编码和可扩展量子网络的实现提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the inter-sideband phase coherence within the resonance fluorescence spectrum of an acoustically modulated quantum dot using a filtered-field formalism for a Mach-Zehnder configuration. We demonstrate that geometric slant of the interferograms provides an indicator of phase coherence that is resilient to environmental white noise. Specifically, noise-induced spectral diffusion reduces the global fringe intensity, while leaving the characteristic inclination strictly invariant. Our findings establish a framework for verifying single-photon coherence between spectral sidebands, essential for frequency-bin encoding and scalable quantum networking in realistic, noisy solid-state architectures.

</details>


### [104] [Breakdown of Charge-Conjugation Symmetry of Disclinations in 2D Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30921)
*Ruslan Yamaletdinov,Mikhail I. Katsnelson,Oleg V. Yazyev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过原子模拟，研究了自由悬浮石墨烯中向错（disclinations）的弹性能、形态和相互作用，发现负向错（negative disclinations）表现出亚线性能量标度、偏离鞍形平衡形态，并导致长程吸引，而正向错则排斥，电荷共轭对称性被打破，且自粘附会放大这些效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向错是二维晶体膜中基本的拓扑缺陷，但其弹性性质尚未被充分探索，尤其是负曲率缺陷的行为和相互作用机制不清晰，这限制了对其稳定性和形态调控的理解。

Method: 采用原子级模拟（atomistic simulations），对自由悬浮石墨烯（作为典型二维晶体）中的正向错和负向错进行系统研究，分析其能量、平衡形态及相互作用，并额外考察自粘附（self-adhesion）折叠膜的影响。

Result: 正向错的能量和形态符合预期；负向错的能量随拓扑电荷呈亚线性增长，平衡形状明显偏离传统鞍形设想。电荷共轭对称性失效导致相互作用定性不同：正向错相互排斥，负向错呈现稳健的长程吸引；自粘附进一步放大这些趋势。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了负曲率缺陷的基本能量景观与正向错有本质区别，为理解石墨烯及其他二维膜的稳定性、自折叠行为和缺陷驱动形态提供了基础。

Abstract: Disclinations are elementary topological defects in two-dimensional (2D) crystalline membranes, yet their elastic properties remain largely unexplored. Using atomistic simulations, we address the energetics, morphology, and interactions of disclinations in free-standing graphene, a prototypical 2D crystal. We find that while disclinations with positive topological charges follow an expected behavior, negative disclinations exhibit sublinear energy scaling with charge as well as equilibrium shape that deviates sharply from the conventional saddle ansatz. This breakdown of charge-conjugation symmetry leads to qualitatively distinct interactions: positive disclinations repel, whereas negative disclinations display a robust long-range attraction. These trends are shown to be further amplified by self-adhesion in folded membranes. Our results uncover a fundamentally different energetic landscape for negative curvature defects and provide a basis for understanding the stability, self-folding behavior, and defect-driven morphology of graphene and other 2D membranes.

</details>


### [105] [Device characterization of Si$/$SiGe double quantum dots using exchange oscillations in Earth's magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31093)
*Holly G. Stemp,Harry Hanlim Kang,Chih Hwan Yang,Gabriel D. Cutter,Frederike Brockmeyer,Patrick J. Strohbeen,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,William D. Oliver*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在无外加磁场的条件下，利用Si/SiGe中残余核自旋产生的本征磁场梯度，实现了双量子点间的交换振荡，并将其用作表征工具，在无需微磁体、动态核极化或预先多轴标定的情况下，将单重态相干时间从T2*≈1.17μs延长至T2^CPMG≈74.8μs（N=70重聚焦脉冲），并实现了数十kHz残余交换相互作用的相位分辨与映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于交换的半导体量子比特家族需要在零磁场下可靠运行，但传统表征环境噪声、残余空闲相互作用和交换相关衰减的方法通常依赖多轴控制标定或刻意设计的磁场梯度。对于混合半导体-超导体系统，磁场会降低超导组件性能，因此亟需一种更简单的零磁场诊断工具。

Method: 利用同位素富集Si/SiGe中残余核自旋产生的本征磁场梯度，在无微磁体、无动态核极化、无需先验多轴标定的情况下，驱动两个量子点间的交换振荡；并采用Carr-Purcell-Meiboom-Gill（CPMG）交换序列进行相干时间延长与噪声表征。

Result: CPMG交换序列将单重态相干时间从T2* = 1.17±0.02 μs显著延长至T2^CPMG = 74.8±1.8 μs（N=70个重聚焦脉冲）；振荡相位可分辨数十kHz量级的残余交换相互作用，并能在(1,1)电荷胞内进行映射。

Conclusion: 本征梯度交换振荡被确立为一种简单、更贴近实际应用的零磁场诊断方法，适用于exchange-only及相关的半导体量子比特编码方式，且便于快速、高通量的器件表征。

Abstract: Exchange-based semiconductor qubits encompass a broad family of encodings constructed from singlet- and triplet-like spin states, several of which are compatible with operation at zero applied magnetic field. Their reliable operation requires characterization of environmental noise, residual idle interactions, and exchange-dependent decay, but this characterization often relies on multi-axis control calibration or deliberately engineered magnetic-field gradients. A simpler zero-applied-field diagnostic is particularly valuable for hybrid semiconductor-superconductor systems, in which magnetic fields can degrade superconducting components. Here, we use the intrinsic magnetic-field gradient produced by residual nuclear spins in isotopically enriched Si/SiGe to implement exchange oscillations between two quantum dots as a characterization tool without a micromagnet, dynamic nuclear polarization, or prior multi-axis calibration. Using Carr-Purcell-Meiboom-Gill exchange sequences, we extend the singlet coherence from $T_2^*=1.17\pm0.02~μ$s to $T_2^{\mathrm{CPMG}}=74.8\pm1.8~μ$s with $N=70$ refocusing pulses. The oscillation phase resolves residual exchange in the tens-of-kilohertz regime and enables it to be mapped across the $(1,1)$ charge cell. These results establish intrinsic-gradient exchange oscillations as a simple, more relevant zero-field diagnostic for exchange-only and related semiconductor qubit encodings that is amenable to rapid, high-throughput device characterization.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [libNLPBE: An Open-Source Python Package for Solving the Non-Linear Poisson-Boltzmann Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28908)
*Jun-Hyeong Kim,Weitao Yang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了开源Python库libNLPBE，用于求解非线性泊松-玻尔兹曼方程（NLPBE），以在密度泛函计算中考虑电解质溶液环境的静电效应，并实现了改进的mDINMH求解器及GPU加速版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电解质溶液中的离子通过静电相互作用显著影响溶质化学性质，但NLPBE求解器在分子电子结构计算中可用性有限，阻碍了相关理论研究和化学设计。

Method: libNLPBE采用密度拟合近似高效计算溶质静电势；开发了改进的阻尼不精确牛顿多重网格法（mDINMH），通过对称预条件子求解牛顿方程，从而兼容针对对称线性算子的鲁棒多重网格方法，并集成代数多重网格以支持广泛网格点；同时提供GPU加速版本。

Result: 成功实现了一个与密度泛函计算结合的NLPBE求解器，可计算电解质环境对Fock矩阵的静电修正项，并支持GPU并行化以提高效率。

Conclusion: libNLPBE提高了NLPBE求解器的可及性，为在电子结构计算中研究离子效应提供了高效、灵活的开源工具。

Abstract: Solvent environments surrounding a solute can significantly alter its chemical properties. These changes become more prominent in electrolyte solutions, where mobile ions largely influence the solute through electrostatic interactions. A theoretical description of these ionic effects will benefit the design of chemistry performed in electrolyte solutions. As a result, the non-linear Poisson-Boltzmann equation (NLPBE) has emerged as an efficient implicit solvent model for electronic structure calculations to address such effects. However, the limited availability of NLPBE solvers for molecular electronic structure calculations greatly hinders theoretical investigation into the ionic effects. To improve accessibility of the NLPBE, we present libNLPBE, an open-source Python library, that solves the NLPBE for molecular systems in combination with density functional calculations, specifically to obtain electrostatic correction terms arising from the electrolyte solution environment for the Fock matrix. The library employs the density fitting (DF) approximation to efficiently calculate solute electrostatic potentials. Furthermore, we develop the modified Damped Inexact Newton Multigrid developed by Holst (mDINMH) method for solving the NLPBE. The mDINMH features a symmetric preconditioner for solving the Newton equation, which enables the use of robust multigrid methods designed for symmetric linear operators. In addition, an algebraic multigrid method has been incorporated into the mDINMH to support a broad range of grid points. We also present a GPU-accelerated version of libNLPBE to leverage parallelization efficiency of GPUs.

</details>


### [107] [Simulating spectrally resolved exciton--exciton-interaction pump--probe spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29041)
*Kateřina Charvátová,Matteo Bruschi,Pavel Malý*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于光谱分辨的激子-激子相互作用泵浦-探测（EEI-PP）五阶非线性信号的微观理论描述，可处理真实谱线形状，适用于大分子体系（如方酸聚合物），并通过路径分解证明光谱分辨对于区分单激子弛豫与多激子湮灭等动力学至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEI-PP直接探测激子-激子湮灭，能研究扩展近等能景观中的激发能量传输，但以往仅对简化体系进行光谱分辨分析，大体系中只分析光谱积分信号，无法区分相互纠缠的单激子和多激子动力学。

Method: 发展了一个微观理论框架，用于计算光谱分辨的EEI-PP响应，包含真实谱线形状，并适用于大型分子系统（如方酸聚合物）；进一步将总信号分解为单个激发路径，以剖析不同动力学贡献。

Result: 揭示了单激子和多激子态结构如何塑造光谱特征和动力学行为；信号分解显示光谱分辨是必要的，能够区分单激子弛豫与激子-激子湮灭这些原本交织的过程。

Conclusion: 该理论为光谱分辨EEI-PP信号提供了可实验比较的模拟工具，强调光谱分辨在解析复杂分子体系中多激子过程的关键作用，有助于深入理解激发能量输运机制。

Abstract: Exciton--exciton-interaction pump--probe spectroscopy (EEI--PP) captures the fifth-order signal of light-matter interaction, directly probing exciton--exciton annihilation. This allows studying excitation energy transport even in extended, nearly isoenergetic landscapes. Although spectrally resolved fifth-order signals have already been explored in simplified cases, in larger systems only spectrally integrated signals have been analyzed. We present a microscopic theoretical description for spectrally resolved EEI--PP response with realistic lineshapes, revealing how single- and multi-exciton state structure shape the spectral features and dynamics. The description is applicable to large molecular systems such as squaraine polymers, facilitating a direct comparison with experimental spectra. By a decomposition of the total signal into individual excitation pathways, we demonstrate that the spectral resolution is needed to distinguish otherwise intertwined single- and multi-exciton dynamics, such as single-exciton relaxation and exciton--exciton annihilation.

</details>


### [108] [Transition states and dynamical bottlenecks in the Li-NC <-> Li-CN isomerizing reaction with the Lagrangian betweenness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29344)
*P. García-Cuadrillero,F. Revuelta,R. M. Benito,F. Borondo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用拉格朗日介数（Lagrangian betweenness）这一源于海洋输送的动力系统工具，研究Li-NC与Li-CN异构化反应的振动动力学，证明其不仅能识别反应过渡态，还能揭示相空间中的流形、岛链和不变环面等不变结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构化反应的振动动力学由相空间中的瓶颈结构控制，传统方法难以统一刻画反应过渡态与其他不变结构。作者希望借助网络科学中的介数中心性思想，将其推广到动力系统中，以更全面地识别动力学瓶颈。

Method: 采用拉格朗日介数方法，该指标源于复杂网络中的介数中心性，通过衡量轨迹在相空间中作为“桥梁”的程度来定位动力学瓶颈。研究以Li-NC与Li-CN异构化反应为模型体系，分析其振动相空间结构。

Result: 拉格朗日介数能够成功识别出反应过渡态——即位于能垒顶部的短寿命状态；同时也能识别出流形、岛链和不变环面等其他动力学瓶颈，这些结构共同主导了体系的复杂振动动力学。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日介数是一种有效的相空间瓶颈识别工具，可统一揭示化学反应中过渡态与其他不变结构，为理解异构化等反应动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the vibrational dynamics that is responsible for Li-NC <-> Li-CN isomerization reaction using the Lagrangian betweenness, a tool recently developed to identify dynamical bottlenecks in the context of oceanographic transport. Following the spirit of the betweenness-centrality metric, which efficiently identifies the nodes that act as bottlenecks in complex networks, the Lagrangian betweenness is similarly capable to identify bottlenecks in dynamical systems. On the one hand, in this paper we show the ability of the Lagrangian betweenness to identify transition states, which are short-lived states that are formed at the top of the energetic barrier that the system must surpass in order to transition the reaction from the (initial) reactants to the (final) products. On the other hand, we also demonstrate that the Lagrangian betweenness is also able to identify other dynamical bottlenecks, and phase-space invariant structures such as manifolds, chains of islands and invariant tori, which are also responsible for the intricate vibrational dynamics of the system.

</details>


### [109] [Site-Specific Vibrational Dynamics to Probe Local and Global Protein Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29805)
*Sena Aydin,Markus Meuwly*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 通过机器学习势函数和波动电荷模型模拟溶菌酶中多个丙氨酸残基上的振动探针（--SCN、--N3、--SNO），发现振动频率波动相关函数（FFCFs）不仅反映局部环境动力学，还能编码蛋白质低频集体运动，且静态贡献与实验定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振动光谱探针可提供蛋白质结构和动力学的位点特异性信息，但探针的振动响应究竟反映局部涨落还是更广泛的蛋白质集体运动尚不清楚；需要系统研究探针位置、局域水化和环境动力学对振动响应的影响。

Method: 将振动探针共价连接到溶菌酶所有丙氨酸残基上；使用基于机器学习的模型描述键合相互作用（再生核模型）和静电相互作用（波动最小分布电荷）；通过频率波动相关函数（FFCFs）表征光谱动力学，并分析不同探针位置的关联。

Result: 许多探针位置的FFCFs在模拟时间尺度上存在不衰减分量，表明存在残余慢动力学和不完整的构象涨落采样；静态贡献大小与先前的实验观测一致，定性验证了模拟；振动响应可超越局部环境，反映低频、集体蛋白运动。

Conclusion: 位点特异性振动探针能够报告超出其直接局部环境的动力学，在合适的位置可提供蛋白质集体运动的信息，为利用振动光谱研究蛋白质全局构象动力学提供了支持。

Abstract: Vibrational spectroscopic probes can provide site-specific information on protein structure and dynamics. In this work, the possibility to relate protein motion with the vibrational response for --SCN, --N$_3$, and --SNO labels covalently linked to all alanine-residues in lysozyme is investigated. Depending on the position of the probe along the polypeptide chain, its local hydration, and the dynamics of its environment, the vibrational response can encode not only local fluctuations but also low-frequency, collective motions of the protein. The spectroscopic probes are described using machine-learning-based models for both bonded interactions, represented by reproducing-kernel models, and electrostatic interactions, represented by fluctuating minimally distributed charges. Spectroscopic dynamics are characterized through frequency fluctuation correlation functions (FFCFs). For many probe locations, the FFCFs contain a non-decaying component on the simulation time scale, indicating residual slow dynamics and incomplete sampling of the underlying conformational fluctuations. The magnitude of these static contributions is consistent with previous experimental observations, providing qualitative validation of the simulations. Overall, the results demonstrate that site-specific vibrational probes can report on dynamics extending beyond their immediate local environment and can, at suitable locations, provide information on collective protein motions.

</details>


### [110] [Accelerated Quantum-Assisted Selected Configuration Interaction via Fast-Annealing-Based Determinant Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30482)
*Hayun Park,Younghun Kwon,Hunpyo Lee*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子辅助的选定组态相互作用（QASCI）方法，结合图基块对角化（GBBD）将FCI哈密顿量分解为独立块，并将行列式选择问题转化为QUBO，用快速退火迭代求解，从而在保持化学精度的同时大幅减少所需Slater行列式数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCI在给定基组下精确但计算成本随自旋轨道数指数增长；SCI虽只保留重要行列式，但反复识别重要行列式仍是主要计算瓶颈。

Method: QASCI将SCI与GBBD结合：GBBD把FCI哈密顿量划分为独立块，每个块内的行列式选择问题被建模为QUBO问题，并通过快速退火迭代求解，以构建SCI空间。

Result: 在STO3G基组下对H8-H18氢链和Li2S进行基准测试：氢链达到化学精度所需行列式比例很小且随n增大而下降；Li2S在远少于全FCI空间的行列式下保持化学精度。对N2使用631G基组，全轨道QASCI用50000个行列式得到的基态能量低于在12个自旋轨道/12个电子活性空间内FCI计算的结果。

Conclusion: QASCI与GBBD结合能显著降低行列式选择的计算成本，同时保持FCI级电子结构计算精度，从而支持在更大轨道空间中进行精确计算。

Abstract: Full configuration interaction (FCI) provides exact electronic structure within a given atomic basis, but its computational cost grows exponentially with the number of spin orbitals. Selected configuration interaction (SCI) methods alleviate this limitation by retaining only the most important Slater determinants. However, the repeated identification of important determinants remains a major computational bottleneck. We present a quantum assisted selected configuration interaction (QASCI) method that combines SCI with graph based block diagonalization (GBBD) of FCI Hamiltonian. The GBBD method partitions FCI Hamiltonian into independent blocks, within which determinant selection problem is formulated as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem. The QUBO problems for selecting determinants to construct SCI space are iteratively solved using a fast annealing approach. We benchmark method on H8-H18 hydrogen chains and Li2S in STO3G basis. For Hn chains, chemical accuracy is achieved while retaining only a small fraction of Slater determinants, and this fraction decreases with increasing n, despite the exponential growth of the FCI Hilbert space. For Li2S, QASCI results remain within chemical accuracy while retaining substantially fewer determinants than the full FCI space. We apply QASCI to N2 using the 631G basis, considering both active orbital and full orbital treatments. The full orbital QASCI calculation, using 50000 determinants, yields a lower ground state energy than an FCI calculation within an active space comprising 12 spin orbitals and 12 electrons. These results demonstrate that the combination of QASCI and the GBBD approach can substantially reduce computational cost of determinant selection while maintaining the accuracy of FCI based electronic structure calculations, thereby enabling accurate calculations in larger orbital spaces.

</details>


### [111] [Frequency-resolved ultrafast electron diffraction: visualizing vibrational dynamics in frequency- and real-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30868)
*Rosalie Tabarie,Simon P. Neville,Michael Schuurman,Kasra Amini*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出频率分辨超快电子衍射（UED）方法，通过对时间依赖的差分对分布函数（ΔPDF）进行傅里叶变换，获得二维频率-距离表示，用于识别光激发后分子结构动力学中的振动模式及其贡献时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快电子衍射能提供光激发后分子结构变化的直接信息，但难以识别具体哪些振动运动贡献于这些动力学过程，需要新的分析方法来分离和归属振动模式。

Method: 对时间分辨的ΔPDF进行傅里叶变换，得到频率-距离二维图谱；应用于丙二烯和1,2-丁二烯在200 nm激发至S1(ππ*)态的模拟电子散射信号（基于已发表的ab initio多重裁剪轨迹），并改变泵浦-探测延迟范围以分析频率分量的时间贡献。

Result: 成功识别C=C伸缩和CCC弯曲振动及其作用的核间距离，分离重叠的CH2振动；甲基取代降低了C=C伸缩和CCC弯曲频率，并在1,2-丁二烯前90 fs引入额外频率分量；还证明了亚20 fs乃至几飞秒时间分辨率对提取频率分量的必要性。

Conclusion: 频率分辨UED能够同时提供光诱导结构动力学中的振动频率、核间距离和反应时间，是一种解析复杂结构动力学的有力工具。

Abstract: Ultrafast electron diffraction (UED) provides direct information on changes in molecular structure following photoexcitation, but identifying the individual vibrational motions contributing to these dynamics remains challenging. Here, we introduce frequency-resolved UED, where Fourier transformation of the time-dependent difference pair distribution function ($Δ$PDF) gives a two-dimensional frequency-distance representation of the structural dynamics. We apply this approach to allene and 1,2-butadiene following photoexcitation to the $S_1(ππ^*)$ state at 200 nm, using electron scattering signals simulated from previously-published ab initio multiple spawning trajectories (S. P. Neville et al., J. Chem. Phys., 2016, 144, 014305). We identify C=C stretching and CCC bending motions and the internuclear distances over which they contribute, and separate overlapping CH$_2$ vibrational motions. By changing the pump-probe delay range used for the Fourier transform, we identify when specific frequency components contribute during the excited-state dynamics. Methyl substitution reduces the C=C stretching and CCC bending frequencies and introduces an additional frequency component in 1,2-butadiene during the first 90 fs. We further show the importance of sub-20-fs, and ultimately few-femtosecond, temporal resolution for retrieving these frequency components. Frequency-resolved UED therefore provides the vibrational frequencies, internuclear distances, and reaction times associated with photoinduced structural dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Direct Evidence of Unconventional Superconductivity in Doped Kagome system RV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28848)
*Avior Almoalem,Yuqing Xing,Iksu Jang,Daniel J. Schultz,Grgur Palle,Andrea N. Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Rafael M. Fernandes,Jörg Schmalian,Vidya Madhavan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过杂质束缚态谱学实验，发现最优掺杂RbV$_3$Sb$_5$的超导态为全能隙且具有两个能量尺度；其杂质束缚态与常规s波和符号反转s$^{\pm}$配对均不相容，而与手性序参量完全一致，表明该材料实现全能隙手性超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome金属的超导配对对称性仍是核心未解问题，主要原因是缺乏能够区分不同序参量的位相敏感实验。

Method: 利用谱成像扫描隧道显微镜对最优掺杂RbV$_3$Sb$_5$（电荷密度波完全被压制）进行杂质束缚态谱学测量，并对比理论计算。

Result: 发现超导态全能隙且具有两个能量尺度；非磁缺陷周围出现粒子-空穴不对称的束缚态；理论对比排除常规s波和s$^{\pm}$配对，支持手性序参量。

Conclusion: 杂质束缚态谱学可作为Kagome材料超导配对对称性的有效位相敏感探针，并强有力表明最优掺杂RbV$_3$Sb$_5$实现全能隙手性超导态。

Abstract: The superconducting pairing symmetry of kagome metals remains a central unresolved question largely because phase-sensitive experiments capable of distinguishing between different possible order parameters have been difficult to implement. The spatial and energetic characteristics of impurity bound states measured by spectroscopic-imaging scanning tunneling microscopy encode information on the superconducting order parameter. Here, we use impurity-bound state spectroscopy to investigate optimally doped RbV$_3$Sb$_{5}$ where the charge-density wave instability is fully suppressed. We find that the superconducting state is fully gapped and exhibits two distinct energy scales, consistent with a multiband order parameter. Atomic-scale spectroscopy around nonmagnetic defects reveals pronounced particle-hole asymmetric bound states. Comparison with theoretical calculations demonstrates that these bound states are incompatible with conventional $s$-wave and sign-changing $s^{\pm}$ pairing. Our calculations also show that the data are fully consistent with a chiral order parameter. Our results establish impurity-bound-state spectroscopy as a powerful phase-sensitive probe of superconductivity in kagome materials and provide strong evidence that optimally doped RbV$_3$Sb$_{5}$ realizes a fully gapped chiral superconducting state.

</details>


### [113] [Superconductivity and Magnetism in Bi-Ni System: From Bulk to Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28855)
*Gabriel Sant'Ana,Yutao Xing,Milton A. Tumelero*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了Bi-Ni体系中两类超导系统：金属间化合物NiBi₃（4 K超导体，正常相存在复杂磁性但不影响超导）和Bi/Ni双层异质结构（呈现时间反演对称破缺与三重态配对等非常规超导证据），并讨论了当前待解决的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导现象（如时间反演对称破缺、三重态配对和近邻效应）长期是物理与材料科学的重要前沿，而传统候选材料（如铀基化合物）制备困难。Bi-Ni体系因样品易制备而成为研究此类现象的理想平台，亟需系统梳理其电子性质研究进展与争议。

Method: 通过详细文献综述方法，分别考察NiBi₃金属间化合物和Bi/Ni双层异质结构的电子性质，重点分析正常相磁性、超导配对对称性、时间反演对称破缺证据以及近邻效应，并归纳未解决的核心科学问题。

Result: NiBi₃具有4 K超导电性，其正常态表现出复杂磁性行为，但磁性并未直接参与超导；而Bi/Ni异质结构显示出强时间反演对称破缺和自旋三重态配对的强烈证据，呈现出非常规复合超导态，且样品制备相比铀基化合物更简便，加速了研究进展。

Conclusion: Bi-Ni体系为探索非常规超导提供了易制备且现象丰富的平台，但对其超导机制、磁性-超导关联及异质结构中非常规态的完整理解仍需进一步研究，以解决当前存在的开放问题。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity with ingredients of magnetism such as time reversal breaking, triplet pairing and proximity effects has been a long time pursued topic in physics and material science as a new source of innovative phenomena and effects to drive further scientific advances and technologies. The Bi-Ni system has emerged as a convenient candidate for the investigation of such phenomenology. Strictly speaking, the term Bi-Ni system refers to two different schemes, first to the NiBi$_{3}$ intermetallic compound, a 4 K superconductor in which a complex magnetism seems to govern electrical properties in the normal phase, but without implication in the superconducting properties. The second Bi-Ni system is not quite a material but a heterostructure formed by a bilayer of Bi and Ni. Here the superconducting phase has to steal the spotlight by showing strong evidence of time-reversal breaking and triplet pairing featuring a complex unconventional state. The ease of preparing the samples, compared to other candidates for such unconventionality, usually Uranium-based compounds, has accelerated the research in this system. Here, we went through a detailed review of the main results about the electronic properties of these two systems as well as a discussion of the main open questions to be solved in order to reach the complete understanding in this topic.

</details>


### [114] [Electronic state of vortices at twin boundaries in a nematic superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28994)
*Keita Goto,Hiroto Adachi,Masanori Ichioka*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在s±d波向列超导体中，利用Bogoliubov-de Gennes理论研究了有无孪晶界时的涡旋局域电子态，发现孪晶界上的单个涡旋由分数涡旋对组成，并伴随弱诱导的s±id波分量，计算结果与FeSe的STM涡旋图像吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解向列超导体中涡旋芯的电子结构，以及孪晶界对涡旋态的影响，是解释铁基超导体FeSe中STM实验观测的关键。

Method: 采用基于紧束缚模型的Bogoliubov-de Gennes理论，通过转移积分各向异性表征向列性，考虑近邻位点间的吸引相互作用，计算s波和d波配对势分量及局域态密度，分析有无孪晶界时涡旋芯结构。

Result: 在零磁场下不出现s±id波分量，但孪晶界上捕获的单个涡旋却由分数涡旋束缚对组成，并伴随弱诱导的s±id波分量；这些局域电子态的空间结构与FeSe的STM涡旋图像一致。

Conclusion: 向列性和孪晶界共同决定了涡旋芯的奇特结构，分数涡旋和s±id波分量的出现为理解FeSe等铁基超导体的涡旋物理提供了重要证据。

Abstract: Local electronic states of vortices in an s $\pm$ d wave nematic superconductor are studied both in the absence and presence of twin boundaries. The Bogoliubov-de Gennes theory for a tight-binding model is used with its nematicity represented by the anisotropy in the transfer integrals and attractive interactions between the nearest-neighbor sites. We evaluate s and d wave components of the pair potentials and the local density of states, and analyze the effects of nematicity on the vortex core structures with/without twin boundaries. We find that a single vortex trapped at the twin boundary is composed of a bound pair of fractional vortices accompanied by weakly-induced s $\pm$ id wave components, despite that such s $\pm$ id wave components do not appear in a zero magnetic field. The calculated spatial structures of the local electronic states are compared with the vortex image measured by STM experiments in an iron-based superconductor FeSe.

</details>


### [115] [Doping-driven evolution of pairing symmetry in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29091)
*Hai-Yang Zhang,Yu-Jie Bai,Fan-Jie Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在 pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 中，未掺杂时最有利的超导配对为 $s_{\pm}$-波，其隙函数在费米口袋间符号反转，源自磁奇模介导的排斥相互作用。空穴掺杂使 $\gamma$ 费米口袋扩大，增强口袋内由磁偶模驱动的排斥作用，并在重空穴掺杂区驱动配对对称性从 $s_{\pm}$-波转变为 $d_{xy}$-波；而电子掺杂即使在 Lifshitz 转变后的重电子掺杂区仍保持 $s_{\pm}$-波配对，表明 $\gamma$ 口袋对超导并非必需，其缺失反而通过 $\alpha$-$\beta$ 口袋间的有利嵌套增强自旋涨落，使 $s_{\pm}$ 配对更稳固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 中超导配对对称性及其随掺杂的演化，以理解双层镍酸盐超导的配对机制，并为实验检验提供理论依据。

Method: 通过分析不同掺杂浓度下的费米面拓扑、配对相互作用（区分磁奇模与偶模贡献）、自旋涨落以及 Lifshitz 转变，系统研究 $s_{\pm}$-波与 $d_{xy}$-波配对之间的竞争和转变条件。

Result: 未掺杂体系最优配对为 $s_{\pm}$-波，由磁奇模排斥相互作用驱动；重空穴掺杂下，$\gamma$ 口袋扩大导致偶模驱动的口袋内排斥占主导，配对转变为 $d_{xy}$-波；电子掺杂下 $s_{\pm}$-波贯穿始终，即使经历 Lifshitz 转变。$\gamma$ 口袋缺失时，$\alpha$-$\beta$ 口袋的有利嵌套增强自旋涨落，反而使 $s_{\pm}$ 配对更稳健。

Conclusion: 掺杂演化揭示出 $s_{\pm}$-波与 $d_{xy}$-波之间的竞争关系，并表明 $\gamma$ 费米口袋并非双层镍酸盐超导的必要条件。这一结果为在 pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 中检验配对机制提供了新途径，强调自旋涨落与费米面嵌套在稳定非常规超导中的核心作用。

Abstract: We investigate the superconducting pairing symmetry and its doping evolution in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. For the undoped compound, the most favorable pairing state is found to be $s_{\pm}$-wave, characterized by sign reversal of the gap functions between the Fermi pockets. A detailed analysis of the pairing interactions reveals that this unconventional state originates from repulsive interactions mediated by the magnetic odd modes of the bilayer nickelate. Upon hole doping, the $γ$ Fermi pocket expands, which amplifies the intrapocket repulsions on this pocket. These repulsions, driven by the magnetic even modes, gradually dominates the pairing interactions and ultimately drive a transition in pairing symmetry from $s_{\pm}$-wave to $d_{xy}$-wave in the heavily hole-doped regime. In stark contrast, the $s_{\pm}$-wave pairing persists under electron doping, even deep into the heavily electron-doped regime where a Lifshitz transition occurs. This finding suggests that the $γ$ Fermi pocket is not essential for the emergence of superconductivity in bilayer nickelates. In fact, the $s_{\pm}$-wave pairing becomes more robust in the absence of the $γ$ pocket, as spin fluctuations are strongly enhanced by the favorable nesting between the $α$ and $β$ pockets --- a condition guaranteed by Luttinger's theorem and the Fermi surface topology. We believe that exploring the doping evolution of superconducting pairing will open a new realm for testing the pairing mechanism in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$.

</details>


### [116] [Topological pair density waves in kagome superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29156)
*Jia-Xin Yin,Xianxin Wu,Mark H. Fischer,Xiao-Yu Yan,Yigui Zhong,Kozo Okazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了拓扑配对密度波（TPDW）的研究进展，探讨其在kagome超导体中的潜在实现，并总结相关实验 signatures 如可切换手性配对调制、Bogoliubov费米态、超导二极管效应和反常热霍尔效应，展望其未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDW是一种有限动量配对导致的周期性配对调制超导态，在二维中多个Q分量的非平庸相对相位可破坏时间反演对称性并产生拓扑电子结构，因此有必要系统梳理TPDW的概念、实现挑战和潜在材料平台。

Method: 从FFLO态出发引入TPDW概念，分析其实现困难；讨论kagome晶格中TPDW与手性超导、环电流及量子反常霍尔效应的联系；结合AV3Sb5材料体系综述实验证据和相关量子效应。

Result: 在AV3Sb5超导体中观察到多种TPDW相关量子效应，包括可切换的手性配对调制、Bogoliubov费米态、超导二极管效应和反常热霍尔效应，支持TPDW的存在及其拓扑特性。

Conclusion: TPDW是关联拓扑量子相的重要范例，将有限动量配对、拓扑物质和手性超导联系起来，未来在材料设计、机制理解和器件应用方面具有广阔研究前景。

Abstract: The pair density wave (PDW) is an unconventional superconducting state exhibiting periodic pairing modulations due to pairing with finite momentum Q. In two dimensions, multiple Q components of the PDW can have a non-trivial relative phase, breaking time-reversal symmetry and resulting in a topological electronic structure. Here we review progress on exploring such topological PDWs (TPDWs) and discuss their potential realization in kagome superconductors. We first introduce the concept of a TPDW starting from the Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) state and examine the challenges toward its realization. We then discuss the possibility of a TPDW in the kagome lattice, which intertwines with chiral superconductivity and loop currents, thus connecting to models describing the quantum anomalous Hall effect. Furthermore, we review the experimental signatures of a TPDW in AV3Sb5 (A = Cs, Rb, K) superconductors and highlight related quantum effects, including switchable chiral pairing modulations, Bogoliubov Fermi states, the superconducting diode effect, and the anomalous thermal Hall effect. Finally, we project the future research opportunities of this correlated topological quantum phase and discuss its broad implications for finite-momentum pairing, topological matter, and chiral superconductivity.

</details>


### [117] [Controlling Intertwined Electronic Orders in FeSe with Exfoliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29407)
*Wenyao Liu,Gabriel Natale,Kyung-Mo Kim,Birender Singh,Piyush Sakrikar,Stephen D. Funni,Leo Kondo,Augustin Davignon,Antoine de Lagrave,Kota Ishihara,Michael Geiwitz,Maia G. Vergniory,Takasada Shibauchi,Jun Sung Kim,Michał Papaj,Judy J. Cha,Kenneth S. Burch†*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究发现，通过机械剥离将非常规超导体FeSe减薄至二维极限，可抑制体相电子向列序，并将超导配对对称性从s波转变为d波主导，为调控量子有序提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调控二维超导体中相互交织的电子有序态是回答基础问题和设计量子器件的关键，但传统方法依赖复杂化学、应变或界面工程，亟需更简洁的调控途径。

Method: 采用机械剥离实现FeSe的维度缩减，结合输运测量、电子显微镜、拉曼光谱表征向列序变化，并通过角度依赖的Andreev反射谱探测超导能隙结构，辅以注入角、磁场、温度依赖实验及理论建模。

Result: 薄层FeSe中向列序显著减弱，超导对称性从体相s波转变为d波主导；随结取向旋转，谱从零能束缚态演变为相干峰，证实了对称性转变。

Conclusion: 维度缩减提供了一种无需外部调控即可切换超导对称性的纯净方法，为工程化量子有序和揭示配对机制开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Controlling intertwined electronic orders in two-dimensional superconductors offers an effective route to answering fundamental questions and engineering new quantum devices. However, tuning the balance between competing orders typically requires complex chemistry, strain, or interface engineering. Here, we show that a pristine alternative is the dimensional reduction of the unconventional superconductor FeSe. Exfoliation suppresses the bulk electronic nematic response and switches the superconducting symmetry from bulk s-wave to d-wave-dominant. Transport, electron microscopy, and Raman spectroscopy establish the substantial weakening of nematic order in thin flakes. To probe superconductivity, we perform angle-dependent Andreev reflection spectroscopy on pristine crystal edges. As the junction's orientation is rotated, the spectra evolve from zero-energy bound states to coherence peaks. The injection angle, field, and temperature dependence, along with theoretical modeling, confirm that exfoliation switches the superconducting symmetry. Our results suggest a versatile superconducting platform for engineering quantum orders and provide fresh insights into the underlying pairing mechanisms.

</details>


### [118] [Competition between on-site and off-site pairing phases and surface spectra in superconducting wallpaper fermion systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29691)
*Kaito Yoda,Ai Yamakage*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 理论研究了拓扑晶体绝缘体表面壁纸费米子中超导配对中on-site与off-site配对的竞争，发现off-site配对通道可产生B2u和Eu主导相，并导致壁纸费米子之间或其与双Majorana Kramers对之间的谱杂交，形成扭曲表面能谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非对称性保护表面态（壁纸费米子）在超导配对下的行为，特别是on-site配对之外off-site配对如何影响配对竞争和表面谱杂交现象。

Method: 基于拓扑晶体绝缘体表面理论，对超导配对对称性分类与竞争进行理论分析，考察最近邻跃迁配对及不同off-site配对通道的稳定性，并分析B2u和Eu配对态的谱杂交效应。

Result: 面内最近邻配对稳定A1u相；off-site配对通道产生B2u和Eu附加主导相。B2u态引起壁纸费米子谱自身杂交，而时间反演不变向列Eu态引起壁纸费米子与双Majorana Kramers对的谱杂交，产生扭曲表面能谱。

Conclusion: off-site配对相互作用下，由杂交产生的扭曲色散关系仍然存在，表明该现象对配对通道具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the competition between on-site and off-site pairings in superconducting topological crystalline insulators hosting wallpaper fermions, a class of surface states protected by nonsymmorphic symmetries. In-plane nearest-neighbor pair hopping stabilizes the $\mathrm{A_{1u}}$ phase, while off-site pairing channels yield additional dominant phases with $\mathrm{B_{2u}}$ and $\mathrm{E_u}$ representations. We further show that the $\mathrm{B_{2u}}$ pairing state supports the hybridization between wallpaper-fermion spectra themselves, whereas a representative time-reversal-invariant nematic $\mathrm{E_u}$ state exhibits spectral hybridization between wallpaper fermion and double Majorana Kramers pairs, yielding twisted surface energy spectra. These results demonstrate that the twisted dispersions arising from hybridization phenomena persist in the presence of off-site pairing interactions.

</details>


### [119] [Magnetic contacts on freestanding superconducting LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ micromembranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29771)
*Thies Jansen,Pinelopi Konstantinopoulou,Niklas Martin,Fabio Miletto Granozio,Alessia Sambri,Rasmus Bjørk,Felix Trier,Thomas S. Jespersen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过侧向接触几何结构，在LAO-STO超导微米膜上集成铁磁镍接触，低温输运证实超导性保持，且磁历史产生可调有效磁场，基于有效场模型可推断接触磁化强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAO-STO界面二维电子气具有超导性，是超导自旋电子学有前景的平台，但将铁磁接触与超导2DEG集成一直具有挑战性。

Method: 采用侧向接触几何结构，将铁磁镍接触与LAO-STO超导微米膜集成，并通过低温输运测量及有效场模型分析。

Result: 超导性在铁磁接触存在下保持，超导态受镍接触磁历史强烈影响，产生可调有效磁场，且可从最大超导响应推断接触磁化强度。

Conclusion: 铁磁接触的LAO-STO确立了用于氧化物超导体自旋注入研究的平台，并为基于复杂氧化物界面的超导自旋电子器件提供了途径。

Abstract: The superconducting two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) at the LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ (LAO-STO) interface is a promising platform for superconducting spintronics, however, integrating ferromagnetic contacts with the superconducting 2DEG remains challenging. Here, we realize superconducting LAO-STO micromembrane devices contacted by ferromagnetic nickel contacts through a side-contact geometry. Low-temperature transport measurements demonstrate that superconductivity is preserved in the presence of the ferromagnetic contacts. We show that the superconducting state is strongly influenced by the magnetic history of the nickel contacts, which generates a tunable effective magnetic field in the 2DEG. Through an effective field model, the magnetization of the contacts can be inferred from the maximum superconducting response. Our results establish ferromagnetically contacted LAO-STO as a platform for future investigations of spin injection into oxide superconductors and provide a route towards superconducting spintronic devices based on complex oxide interfaces.

</details>


### [120] [Interplay of Excitonic Charge Density Wave and Superconductivity in Transition Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29822)
*Ayussh Anubhav Patel,Amit Ghosal*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过在二维三角晶格上建立包含两个轨道的激子电荷密度波与超导相互作用的最小模型，并在平均场框架下分析，揭示了1T-TiSe2中CDW随调控逐渐减弱并转变为不相称、随后超导出现的普适相图，且相图对能带细节不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-TiSe2的相图具有独特特征，实验上发现插层、压力、替代和门控等调控会导致CDW逐渐减弱并出现超导，且CDW从相称变为不相称。理解这一CDW与超导复杂交织的机制是关键动机。

Method: 基于观察到的激子CDW以及费米面上的电子和空穴口袋，在二维三角晶格上构建每个位点含两个轨道的最小模型，并采用简单平均场框架进行分析。

Result: 模型能够理解若干重要实验特征，并发现定性相图对能带结构细节不敏感，CDW减弱、不相称化与超导出现的演化具有普适性。

Conclusion: 这些结果为理解过渡金属硫族化合物中CDW与超导相互作用的本质提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Motivated by the unique characteristics of the phase diagram of 1T-TiSe2, we investigated the complex interplay of excitonic charge density wave (CDW) and superconductivity (SC) on a two-dimensional triangular lattice, each site accommodating two orbitals. In response to various tuning parameters, such as intercalation, pressure, substitution, and gating, these materials exhibit a generic and gradual waning of CDW, followed by the emergence of SC. Intriguingly, the nature of CDW changes from commensurate to incommensurate with the appearance of SC. Setting up a minimal model based on observed excitonic CDW and electron and hole pockets in the Fermi surface, and analyzing it within a simple mean-field framework, we can comprehend some salient experimental features. We also found that the qualitative phase diagram remains insensitive to details of the band structure. These results offer crucial insights into the nature of the interplay between CDW and SC in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [121] [Ballistic-to-Localized Dynamics as Signature of Quantum Phase Transition in Josephson Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29830)
*F. G. Capone,A. de Candia,G. Di Bello,V. Cataudella,R. Fazio,N. Nagaosa,C. A. Perroni,G. De Filippis*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过数值方法研究小电容约瑟夫森结在欧姆、亚欧姆和超欧姆耗散下的行为，发现欧姆耗散驱动BKT量子相变，超欧姆无相变，亚欧姆为连续二级相变；欧姆区不存在扩散-局域化交叉，而是弹道-局域化转变，并在有限频率下出现长寿命激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子相位涨落和准粒子隧穿如何在不同耗散机制下塑造小电容约瑟夫森结的行为，并澄清此前关于相变和动力学交叉的争议。

Method: 采用最先进的数值技术，结合实频率线性响应计算，分析不同耗散强度下的热平衡相变与动力学响应。

Result: 欧姆耗散增强驱动BKT量子相变；超欧姆无相变，亚欧姆表现连续二级相变，与自旋玻色子模型普适类一致。欧姆区中电阻仅抑制零频奇异响应，引发弹道到局域化的动力学转变；有限频率下环境耦合产生长寿命电荷激发，随电阻降低演化为共振。

Conclusion: 耗散偏离欧姆行为会根本改变相变图景，且欧姆区动力学非传统的扩散-局域化交叉，为约瑟夫森结量子耗散行为提供了新认识。

Abstract: Using state-of-the-art numerical techniques, we investigate how quantum phase fluctuations and quasiparticle tunneling shape the behavior of a small-capacitance Josephson junction across Ohmic, sub-Ohmic, and super-Ohmic dissipation regimes. We show that increasing the Ohmic dissipation strength drives a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless quantum phase transition at thermodynamic equilibrium. Deviations from Ohmic behavior profoundly alter this scenario: the super-Ohmic regime exhibits no phase transition, whereas the sub-Ohmic regime displays a continuous second-order transition, consistent with the universality classes of the spin-boson model. Within the Ohmic regime, real-frequency linear-response calculations reveal that the phase particle does not undergo the commonly assumed diffusive-to-localized crossover. Instead, finite resistance progressively suppresses the singular zero-frequency response, producing a ballistic-to-localized change in the dynamics. At finite frequencies, coupling to the environment generates a long-lived excitation in the charge response, which evolves into a resonance as the subgap and shunt resistances are reduced.

</details>


### [122] [Superheating Field of Extreme Type-II Superconductors Calculated from the Eliashberg Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29842)
*Ramesh Paudel,Alex Gurevich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于各向同性Eliashberg理论，计算了大Ginzburg-Landau参数第二类超导体的超heating场H_s，研究了非磁杂质散射参数p从清洁到脏极限对H_s(T)的影响，并以Nb和Nb_3Sn为例给出具体数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超heating场H_s决定了超导体在磁场中的亚稳态临界行为，但以往多基于弱耦合BCS模型；需要更真实的强耦合Eliashberg理论来评估材料依赖和杂质散射对H_s的影响。

Method: 采用热力学方法，从各向同性Eliashberg理论出发，结合实验隧穿提取的电子-声子谱函数，对不同非磁杂质散射参数p（覆盖清洁极限p≪1到脏极限p≫1）计算H_s(T,p)，并与热力学临界场H_c比较。

Result: Eliashberg理论给出的H_s(T,p)/H_c(T)高于弱耦合BCS模型；尽管非磁杂质在载流态下是pairbreakers，Nb_3Sn在T=0时H_s(0,p)在p≈0.2处出现极大值，且H_s/H_c比BCS清洁极限高约6%。

Conclusion: 强耦合效应和有限非磁杂质散射可显著提升超heating场的相对大小，说明在评估超导射频腔或超导加速器材料性能时，必须超越BCS弱耦合近似并考虑杂质散射参数的影响。

Abstract: We calculate the superheating field $H_s$ of a type-II superconductor with a large Ginzburg-Landau parameter from the isotropic Eliashberg theory. It is shown that the temperature dependencies of $H_s(T)$ can be obtained from a thermodynamic approach for different scattering parameters $p=\hbar/2πτ_Nk_BT_c$ in a wide range of the nonmagnetic impurity scattering times $τ_N$ ranging from the clean $(p\ll 1)$ to the dirty $(p\gg 1)$ limits. Specific calculations were done for Nb and Nb$_3$Sn using their electron-phonon spectral functions extracted from tunneling measurements. We show that the ratio $H_s(T,p)/H_c(T)$ calculated from the Eliashberg theory exceeds that of the weak coupling BCS model, where $H_c$ is the thermodynamic magnetic field. We also show that, even though nonmagnetic impurities are pairbreakers in the current-carrying state, $H_s(0,p)$ for Nb$_3$Sn at $T=0$ has a maximum at $p\approx 0.2$ at which the ratio $H_s/H_c$ is about $6\%$ higher than that in the BCS clean limit.

</details>


### [123] [Chemical Perspectives on Cuprate Superconductivity: Hole-Protected Spin Dimers as d Wave Cooper Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30001)
*Myung-Hwan Whangbo,Reinhard K. Kremer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出铜氧化物超导体中每个d波库珀对是一个被反铁磁空穴二聚体（AHD）保护的反铁磁耦合自旋二聚体，AHD链提供自旋二聚体无扰动集体运动的通道，并以此自然解释若干困惑性实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体的相干长度极短，促使作者探究其实空间结构；作者希望从CuO4单元空穴掺杂的角度理解超导配对机制，并解释实验中看似矛盾的观测现象。

Method: 作者分析空穴掺杂CuO4单元在空穴掺杂CuO2层中发生相分离的聚集体，引入“偏析压力指数”（segregation-pressure indices）进行定量表征，基于此提出AHD及自旋二聚体通道模型，并检验其对多个观测事实的解释能力。

Result: 研究表明每个d波库珀对对应一个受AHD保护的反铁磁耦合自旋二聚体，AHD链形成允许自旋二聚体集体移动的通道；该模型能够自然解释多个关于铜氧化物超导体的令人困惑的实验现象。

Conclusion: 作者认为所提出的AHD机制可能揭示铜氧化物超导体实空间配对结构的关键，为理解其反常物理性质提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: The observation that the coherence lengths in cuprate superconductors are extremely small motivated us to search for its real-space structure by examining the consequence of hole-doping in CuO4 units in cuprates. We analyzed aggregates of hole-doped CuO4 units that phase-separate from hole-doped CuO2 layers in terms of their segregation-pressure indices defined in this work. Our search suggests that each d-wave Cooper pair is an antiferromagnetically coupled spin dimer protected in an antiferromagnetic hole dimer (AHD), and that the antiferromagnetic chains of AHDs provide channels through which such spin dimers can move collectively without perturbation. Based on these suggestions and their implications, we examined the causes for several seemingly puzzling observations on cuprate superconductors to find that they are naturally explained by the suggestions.

</details>


### [124] [Twist-Tunable Paramagnetic Superconductivity in $d$-wave Altermagnet/Superconductor Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30010)
*Narges Kia,Saeed H. Abedinpour,Zahra Faraei*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 理论研究了d波超导体与二维d波交错磁体构成的扭转异质结，发现超导能隙节点与交错磁自旋劈裂节点的动量失配可产生鲁棒且可扭角调控的奇频自旋三重态配对，并使系统宏观电磁响应在常规抗磁Meissner态与反常顺磁Meissner效应之间转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转角工程与非传统磁性（如交错磁）的结合为调控量子现象提供了新途径，但此前尚未系统探索d波超导体与d波交错磁体异质结中节点失配如何影响配对对称性和宏观超导响应。

Method: 通过理论建模分析d波超导体与二维d波交错磁体近邻异质结，考察超导能隙节点与交错磁自旋劈裂节点的动量空间失配，并计算不同界面扭角下的奇频自旋三重态配对及宏观电磁响应。

Result: 奇频自旋三重态配对具有鲁棒性和扭角可调性；对于d_{x^2-y^2}交错磁体，顺磁Meissner响应在完美对齐φ=0时最大，在φ=π/4时完全抑制；对于d_{xy}交错磁体则呈现完全互补的行为。

Conclusion: 扭转交错磁异质结构为工程化奇频配对和调控宏观超导相提供了一个多功能平台，有望推动新型超导量子器件的设计。

Abstract: The interplay between twist-angle engineering and unconventional magnetism provides a powerful new route to control quantum phenomena. We theoretically investigate a heterostructure comprising a $d$-wave superconductor proximitized by a two-dimensional $d$-wave altermagnet. We reveal that the momentum-space mismatch between the superconducting gap nodes and the altermagnetic spin-splitting nodes generates a robust, twist-tunable odd-frequency spin-triplet pairing. Consequently, the macroscopic electromagnetic response of the system can be tuned from a conventional diamagnetic Meissner state to an anomalous paramagnetic Meissner effect driven entirely by the interfacial twist angle. For a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ altermagnet, the paramagnetic response is maximized at perfect alignment ($φ=0$) and completely suppressed at a maximal twist of $φ=π/4$, while a $d_{xy}$ altermagnet exhibits the exact complementary behavior. Our results establish twisted altermagnetic heterostructures as a versatile platform for engineering odd-frequency pairing and macroscopic superconducting phases.

</details>


### [125] [Superconductivity of Tellurium Polyhydride with Tc above 90K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30139)
*Jinfu Zhu,Guiqi Liu,Yuanhao Su,Hongyu Liu,Sijia Zhang,Panpan Kong,Qingqing Liu,Jianfa Zhao,Shaomin Feng,Jun Zhang,Haoyu Zheng,Jing Song,Luhong Wang,Fuyang Liu,Haozhe Liu,M. Bykov,Xiancheng Wang,Changqing Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 实验发现碲多氢化物TeH4在263 GPa高压下具有约91 K的超导临界温度，成为继SH3之后又一硫族多氢化物高温超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型多氢化物高温超导体，尤其是硫族元素（如Te）的多氢化物，以扩展高压氢基超导材料家族。

Method: 利用金刚石压砧结合激光加热系统在高温高压条件下合成Te多氢化物，通过原位输运测量（变温、加磁场）确定超导转变，并利用同步辐射X射线衍射表征晶体结构。

Result: 观察到Tc约91 K（263 GPa）的超导转变，确定超导相为TeH4，其结构包含共面TeH12笼和类分子H2单元，Ginzburg-Landau相干长度约47 Å。

Conclusion: TeH4是新型硫族多氢化物超导体，丰富了高压氢基超导体系，为理解多氢化物超导机制提供了新例证。

Abstract: We report experimental diacovery of superconductivity (SC) in tellurium (Te) polyhydride. The compound was synthesized at high pressure and high temperature conditions using a diamond anvil cell combined with a laser heating system. Subsequent in situ transport measurements at high pressures, performed as a function of temperature and applied magnetic field, revealed a superconducting transition with a critical temperature Tc about 91 K at 263 GPa. The superconducting phase is assigned to TeH4 with characterized face shared TeH12 cage forming quasi molecular H2 units based on synchrotron x-ray diffraction experiments. Analysis of the SC behavior at magnetic fields yielded a Ginzburg Landau (GL) coherence length of approximately 47 angstroms. Tellurium polyhydride thus becomes another chalcogen polyhydride superconductor in addition to the landmark discovery of the first polyhydride high Tc SC SH3.

</details>


### [126] [Microwave-Induced Optomagnetism in High-Temperature Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31127)
*Anoop Dhillon,Amir Borji,Hamed Majedi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 首次实验观察到高温超导体中稳态微波驱动的逆法拉第效应：圆偏振微波在YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋δ薄膜中产生依赖螺旋度的光磁响应，转换效率高达1.75 T/(W cm⁻²)，并伴随涡旋相滑移与模式锁定谐波行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导体系中微波频段逆法拉第效应的存在与机制，并寻求超越光学频段效率的接触式非感应磁场控制及非平衡涡旋谱学新途径。

Method: 在外延YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋δ薄膜上施加圆偏振微波辐射，利用同相霍尔输运检测螺旋度依赖的响应；通过对比正常态、线性偏振、不同功率及高场条件，表征其超导关联性和涡旋动力学特征。

Result: 光电响应仅在T_c以下出现，正常态消失；有效光磁转换达1.75 T/(W cm⁻²)，远超光学基准；高功率下自生场超过B_c1时信号坍缩，随后在涡旋洗板谐波处重新出现。

Conclusion: 稳态微波光磁效应为超导量子系统提供了一种无需接触、非感性的磁场操控方式，并开启非平衡涡旋谱学的新研究方向。

Abstract: We report the first experimental observation of a steady-state, microwave-driven inverse Faraday effect in a high-temperature superconductor. Circularly polarized microwave radiation generates a helicity-dependent response in an epitaxial $\mathrm{YBa_2Cu_3O_{7-δ}}$ film, detected using homodyne Hall transport. The optomagnetic response emerges exclusively below $T_c$, vanishes in the normal state, and exhibits no power-dependent counterpart under linearly polarized excitation. The effective optomagnetic conversion reaches $1.75\,\mathrm{T}/(\mathrm{W\,cm^{-2}})$, surpassing optical benchmarks by several orders of magnitude. At higher microwave powers, the signal collapses when the self-generated field exceeds $B_{c1}$, marking the onset of a vortex phase-slip regime, and subsequently re-emerges at mode-locked vortex-washboard harmonics. These results establish steady-state microwave optomagnetism as a route to contactless, non-inductive magnetic control and nonequilibrium vortex spectroscopy in superconducting quantum systems.

</details>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Characteristic Mode Analysis of Composite Nanostructures using a Coupled System of Volume Integral and Hydrodynamic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29709)
*Meruyert Khamitova,Ran Zhao,Doolos Aibek Uulu,Sebastian Celis Sierra,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 针对金属-介质复合纳米结构，提出基于体积积分方程与流体动力学方程耦合系统的全结构与子结构特征模分析（CMA）公式。子结构CMA通过消去介质区未知量构建降阶系统，仅用金属区电流表示，效率更高，且能识别相同谐振（若介质无谐振）；两种公式均经消光截面验证，用于表征介质环境对金属谐振的调谐与杂化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMA主要针对纯金属或纯介质结构，难以高效且物理清晰地分析金属-介质复合纳米结构中金属谐振如何受介质环境影响；需发展可分离金属区本征响应并考虑介质作用的CMA方法。

Method: 基于耦合的VIE和HDE建立金属-介质复合纳米结构全波模型；全结构CMA直接对完整耦合系统矩阵构造广义特征方程；子结构CMA则先消去介质未知数得到仅含金属电流的降阶系统，再对该系统构造广义特征方程，从而在保留介质影响的同时降低计算维度。

Result: 两公式均与消光截面（ECS）结果一致；子结构CMA在介质无谐振时可识别与全结构相同的金属谐振且计算更高效；可用于揭示衬底引起的红移，以及高对比度直接接触衬底时产生的模态杂化响应。

Conclusion: 子结构CMA为金属-介质复合纳米结构提供高效而准确的谐振分析方法，能够清晰分离并表征金属本征模式在介质环境中的频移和杂化，适用于复杂衬底及复合材料的光学特性研究。

Abstract: Full-structure and sub-structure characteristic mode analysis (CMA) formulations are developed for composite metallic--dielectric nanostructures based on a coupled system of volume integral equations (VIE) and the hydrodynamic equation (HDE). In the full-structure CMA, the generalized eigenvalue equation (GEE) is constructed from the matrix of the complete coupled system, and the resulting characteristic currents describe the response of the entire composite nanostructure. In the sub-structure CMA, the GEE is constructed from a reduced system, derived from the coupled system by eliminating the dielectric-region unknowns, so that it is expressed only in terms of the metallic-region currents. This isolates the resonances of the metallic region while still incorporating the effect of the dielectric region through the reduced system. Because the reduced system has a smaller dimension, the sub-structure CMA is computationally more efficient than the full-structure CMA and, when the dielectric does not resonate in the frequency range of interest, identifies the same resonances. Both formulations are validated against extinction cross-section (ECS) results and are used to characterize how a dielectric environment reshapes the metallic resonances, including substrate-induced red-shifts and, for high-contrast substrates in direct contact, hybridized modal responses.

</details>


### [128] [A wide-range temperature-dependent deep potential for sodium with near-experimental accuracy from melting to the critical point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29826)
*G. S. Demyanov,D. V. Minakov,V. B. Fokin,P. R. Levashov*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 利用有限温度DFT数据构建了钠的温度相关深度势，系统研究了从室温到临界区的热物理性质，其中r²SCAN泛函模型预测的临界参数与推荐值高度一致，并进一步计算了热力学性质与输运性质，验证了结果的热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钠在高温、近临界区的热物理性质（如临界参数、输运性质）实验数据稀少且难以直接测量，而传统势函数模型在宽温度范围下精度有限。因此需要构建高精度、温度相关的机器学习势能模型，并评估不同交换关联泛函对预测结果的影响。

Method: 采用PBE、AM05和r²SCAN三种交换关联泛函生成有限温度DFT数据，据此训练温度相关的深度势（Deep Potential）模型。通过直接固液共存模拟获取熔点，液汽共存模拟复现双节线和表面张力，并采用声学模拟与状态方程对比验证热力学一致性，同时计算多种热力学性质（焓、热容、热膨胀系数、体模量、Grüneisen参数、声速）及输运性质（自扩散系数、剪切粘度）。

Result: r²SCAN模型给出临界温度2.508 kK、密度0.203 g/cm³、压力0.249 kbar，与推荐值接近；正常压力下bcc密度与实验仅差0.06%；熔点为346 K，比推荐值低25 K，符合meV/原子自由能误差的预期；液汽共存模拟复现binodal且表面张力在临界点趋零；自扩散系数和剪切粘度数据扩展至膨胀液体和近临界区；声学模拟声速与状态方程计算结果一致。

Conclusion: 基于r²SCAN泛函的温度相关深度势可以准确描述钠从室温到临界区的热物性，为缺乏实验数据的膨胀液体和近临界区提供可靠预测，同时验证了方法的泛函依赖性和热力学一致性。

Abstract: We construct temperature-dependent deep potentials for sodium using finite-temperature DFT data obtained with the PBE, AM05, and $r^2$SCAN exchange-correlation functionals and investigate the thermophysical properties of sodium from room temperature to the critical region. The predicted critical parameters show a dependence on the exchange-correlation functional. The model based on the $r^2$SCAN functional gives a critical temperature $T_c=2.508(8)$ kK, density $ρ_c=0.203(4)$ g/cm$^{3}$, and pressure $P_c=0.249(6)$ kbar, in close agreement with the recommended values. This model is then used to reconstruct the normal-pressure and critical isobars and to calculate the enthalpy, heat capacities, thermal expansion coefficient, bulk moduli, Grüneisen parameter, and speed of sound. The calculated normal-pressure bcc density differs by only 0.06% from the experimental value. Direct solid-liquid coexistence simulations give a melting temperature of $346(2)$ K, 25 K below the recommended value, consistent with the sensitivity expected from meV/atom free-energy errors. Liquid-vapor coexistence simulations reproduce the binodal and yield a surface tension that approaches zero near the critical point. The calculated self-diffusion coefficient and shear viscosity extend the available transport-property data into the expanded-liquid and near-critical regions, where direct experimental information is sparse. We also demonstrate the thermodynamic consistency of the results by comparing the speed of sound obtained from direct acoustic simulations with that calculated from the equation of state.

</details>


### [129] [Cartesian tensor equivariant machine-learning force field for spin-dependent atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30338)
*Junjie Wang,Yijie Zhu,Zhongwei Zhang,Zhiyue Guo,Xudong Zhu,Lixin He,Chi Ding,Jian Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: HotPP-Spin是一种基于笛卡尔张量等变消息传递的磁机器学习原子间势扩展，通过显式处理原子磁矩为轴向矢量并传播空间反演和时间反演奇偶性，统一描述交换主导和自旋轨道耦合诱导的磁性相互作用，能够在保持能量守恒的同时实现磁性能量景观、磁力及磁序相关能量-体积关系的准确预测，并在单层VSe₂中复现了实验居里温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性材料中原子结构与自旋自由度之间存在复杂耦合，使得跨实验相关长度和时间尺度的原子模拟面临根本性挑战，迫切需要一种能统一描述交换主导和自旋轨道耦合诱导相互作用且无需预设解析形式的机器学习原子间势。

Method: 在HotPP框架上扩展出自旋依赖版本，采用笛卡尔张量等变消息传递，将原子磁矩视为显式轴向矢量自由度，并通过张量耦合传播空间反演和时间反演奇偶性，从而构建标量自旋依赖势能面，再通过微分获得能量守恒的原子力和磁有效场。

Result: 在共线磁性、非共线磁性和自旋轨道耦合致磁各向异性基准测试中，HotPP-Spin在统一框架内准确描述了磁性能量景观、磁力及磁序相关能量-体积关系；对H相单层VSe₂，随机自旋动力学模拟得到的有限尺寸磁序交叉温度为415–435 K，与实验值418.5±7.8 K高度吻合。

Conclusion: 笛卡尔张量消息传递为将第一性原理磁性能学与大规模结构-自旋耦合现象的原子模拟相连接提供了一条通用途径。

Abstract: Magnetic materials exhibit an intricate coupling between atomic structure and spin degrees of freedom, posing a fundamental challenge for atomistic simulations across experimentally relevant length and time scales. Here we introduce HotPP-Spin, a spin-dependent extension of HotPP for magnetic machine learning interatomic potentials, built on Cartesian tensor equivariant message passing. Atomic magnetic moments are treated as explicit axial-vector degrees of freedom, while spatial-inversion and time-reversal parities are propagated through the tensor couplings. This construction provides a unified representation of exchange-dominated and spin-orbit-induced interactions without imposing predefined analytical interaction forms. A scalar spin-dependent potential energy surface yields energy-conserving atomic forces and magnetic effective fields through differentiation. Benchmarks spanning collinear magnetism, noncollinear magnetism, and spin-orbit-coupling-induced magnetic anisotropy show that HotPP-Spin accurately describes magnetic energy landscapes, magnetic forces, and magnetic-order-dependent energy-volume relations within the same general framework. For H-phase monolayer VSe\(_2\), stochastic spin-dynamics simulations using the learned magnetic effective fields locate the finite-size magnetic ordering crossover at 415--435~K, in close numerical agreement with the reported experimental value of \(418.5\pm7.8\)~K. These results establish Cartesian tensor message passing as a general route for connecting first-principles magnetic energetics with large-scale atomistic simulations of coupled structural and spin phenomena.

</details>


### [130] [OpenMUSTANC (MUltiple Scattering Theory At Nanoplasmonic Cavities): A MATLAB toolbox for the simulation of Plasmonic Sphere Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30565)
*Xin Zheng,Christos Mystilidis,Christos Tserkezis,Guy A. E. Vandenbosch,Xuezhi Zheng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MATLAB的计算介观电磁学工具箱，用于模拟任意相对位置的多球面界面体系，集成了HDM、GNOR和SRM等非局域介观模型，支持多种激发源和丰富的后处理功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米结构中的非局域效应需要新的计算电磁学算法或对传统算法的更新，而现有工具难以有效处理介观尺度下的这类问题，因此亟需一个通用、高效的数值工具箱来支持纳米等离子体研究。

Method: 利用矢量球谐函数展开电磁场，采用S矩阵公式高效处理球面界面的入射和散射场，并借助平移矩阵处理不同展开中心之间的传播波；构建模块化例程，分别处理场求解、激发源（平面波、偶极子、电子束）以及后处理计算。

Result: 成功开发了一个模块化、高效且通用的MATLAB工具箱，能够模拟任意相对位置的多球面结构，集成多种介观模型，并实现散射截面、远/近场分布、荧光增强因子、Purcell因子、量子产率、阴极发光及电子能量损失谱等计算。

Conclusion: 该工具箱为纳米等离子体领域提供了一种高度灵活和高效的计算工具，并给出了完整的方法说明和详细使用指南，便于研究者采用。

Abstract: Mesoscopic physical models, including the Hydrodynamic Drude Model (HDM), the Generalized Nonlocal Optical Response (GNOR) Model, and the Surface Response Model (SRM), have been proposed to investigate nonlocal effects in nanometric structures. The combination of classical electromagnetism with these mesoscopic material models calls for new computational electromagnetic (CEM) algorithms, or update of conventional ones, in what is termed computational mesoscopic electromagnetics (CMEM). In this work, we present a MATLAB toolbox for the simulation of multiple spherical interfaces with arbitrary relative positions, with the incorporation of the aforementioned mesoscopic models. The method exploits vector spherical wave functions to properly express electric and magnetic fields, an S matrix formulation for the efficient treatment of incident and scattered fields at spherical interfaces, and a translation matrix to deal with propagating waves with different expansion centers. Excitation sources can be chosen among arbitrarily polarized plane waves, dipoles and electron beams. For the post-processing part, the calculation of cross sections and far/near-field mapping; fluorescence enhancement, Purcell factor and quantum yield; and cathodoluminescence and electron energy-loss probability is implemented. The toolbox is built in a modular manner, and each part (routine) has its own important functionality. This paper provides a full explanation of the proposed highly efficient and general toolbox, and a detailed guideline for researchers in the nanoplasmonics community.

</details>
